## 1. What V3 must do — owner-accepted contract
**Data:** Dhan API only. Oracle does OAuth renewal / authenticated SSK gateway / bounded pacing / technical transport cache; app independently owns Watchlists, timeframes, filters, Smart Control, scan priority and visual state. No Angel One/Yahoo trading/source fallback. Up to four phones, Master access safeguards; no assumptions about unlimited broker calls. A single protected request/response pipe is not proof of Dhan push streaming.

**Universe:** Up to **70 unique equities globally**, across Watchlists without duplicates; three separate indices NIFTY, BANKNIFTY, SENSEX; chosen 5–70 equities should scale requests to selected count, not fixed 70. Test 54 or 73 *instruments* is distinct from 54 or 70 *equities*. Index mapping NIFTY IDX_I/13; BANKNIFTY IDX_I/25; SENSEX BSE_I/51; FINNIFTY optional after three defaults. Picker order NIFTY > BANKNIFTY > SENSEX. Never change mapping by guesswork.

**Timeframe/UI:** Dashboard and main market directional panels default **1m**; Watchlist **3m**; Scanner selection includes 1m/2m/3m/5m and supported 10m/15m/30m/1h. Preserve saved selections. Selected timeframe, provider generation, instrument, expiry and cache must be isolated. Derive 3m from complete source-validated 1m groups where appropriate, never take 5m cache as 1m. Last 2–4 prior overlapping candles may receive corrected OHLCV within same timeframe and series; no fabricated or gapped candles. Quote freshness, candle historical status, UI repaint interval, and broker provider latency are **different clocks**.

**Filters/Engine:** Scanner has **18 supported selectable filters** and retains all saved choices, not old 15-slot truncation. The *five evidence engine chips* and reserved sixth inert display chip are different from those 18 Scanner filters. OI Support, OI Resistance, PCR, Max Pain, candle trend and News have evidence gates. Missing/invalid quote, OI/News or completed history => explicit WAIT/HIST/UNVERIFIED, never a live bullish/bearish vote. Three-move user rule where applicable. No fake Dhan quotes/options or live orders.

**Refresh/UX:** Do not lose green/red candles after page navigation/scroll; never remount whole page or trigger Dhan request simply by scrolling. AUTO scan must remain visible and work after login/resume and respect selected symbol/timeframe and default analysis target **60s** (selector 30/60/120). A target is not a guaranteed complete 70-share fetch cadence; broker pacing still applies. Retry Data, index/equity/Scanner/Watchlist adapters need independently diagnosed route logic. Resume must not wipe verified history or restart entire 51-equity scan; screen-off uninterrupted monitoring is NOT accepted without verified foreground service.

**Doctor/phone test:** Login first, tap `REFRESH DIAGNOSTICS`, then `TEST 54` or `TEST 70`. No stale report before refresh; avoid extra 54/70 live broker fanout. Error-only report: healthy rows summarized as counts; each actual failure includes symbol, timeframe, expiry, source/start, gateway route/status, blocked stage, cache endpoint, error code/class, last good and next action. Specific SENSEX read-only opt-in quote+1m probe after scanner idle; at most one each; positive Dhan result allowed only as **unverified** quote `quote_timestamp=None`, `live_verified=False`. Diagnose News/OI/PCR/Max Pain without inventing active votes.

## 2. Most important user-reported REAL PHONE failures (OPEN until new APK proof)
1. Index: SENSEX price/historical 1m missing or intermittent; NIFTY and BANKNIFTY prices sometimes appear but their red/green candles absent or regress. Source mapping known but Dhan entitlement, request and phone-cache stage still to prove with opt-in SENSEX diagnostic.
2. Watchlist: RELIANCE or other equities showed candle colors, then vanished/reset; after manual Refresh some slowly loaded, sometimes only a single symbol. Need last-good history, source/timeframe isolation and predictable render.
3. Scanner: Auto Scan 30s previously ineffective, user switched BANKNIFTY to RELIANCE and pressed Retry Data but Scan/Filter controls disappeared and scan did not start. Preserve visible AUTO/manual controls and saved symbol/TF.
4. Options/News: OI, PCR, Max Pain and News features at times don't show data or WAIT despite price/candles; separate actual unavailable Dhan/Oracle evidence from broken UI/control adapters. Don't award live votes without verified values.
5. Device UX: scrolling may trigger redundant refresh/rebuild, app can become sluggish; Android inactive/paused/resumed behavior, background monitor and auto mode unexpectedly resetting must be reproduced on phone.
6. Capacity: full 54/70 real cached quote/history/filters on device and end-to-end network latency still NOT verified, despite offline synthetic benchmark.

### 2026-11-21 — SENSEX LEGACY ROUTE INTERFERENCE HYPOTHESIS: TWO-SIDED AUDIT
- Owner hypothesizes V3 SENSEX may still request V1 `/dhan/index-ltp-batch` or the Oracle reverse-proxy might route `/clone-v2/...` to legacy V1 and disturb SENSEX. Audited exact current active branch files `src/main.py`, `src/clone_v2_gateway.py`, `backend/clone_v2_gateway.py`, `ops/clone_v2_nginx_location.conf`, `ops/clone_primary_legacy_dhan_callback_nginx.conf` and `ops/cutover_clone_primary_existing_dhan.sh` (latest relevant source only).
- **V3 phone source evidence:** `src/main.py` explicitly creates `dhan_feed=CloneDhanReadOnly(clone_v2_gateway)` (not `ServerDhanReadOnly`). Index worker calls `dhan_feed.index_batch_quotes()`, optional missing-index `dhan_feed.quote(symbol)`; dedicated SENSEX full probe calls `dhan_feed.intraday("SENSEX",1,...)`. `CloneDhanReadOnly._fetch` invokes `CloneV2Gateway.market_quotes` at **`/clone-v2/market/quotes`**, and `intraday` invokes `CloneV2Gateway.post_market("candles",...)` at **`/clone-v2/market/candles`**. `CloneDhanReadOnly._call` explicitly raises `Unsupported Clone B3 market request` instead of falling back to old `/dhan/...`. Thus **no V1 HTTP endpoint used by SENSEX current V3 data code**.
- `src/main.py` still contains legacy string `/dhan/index-ltp-batch` as a key to `dhan_feed.cooldown_remaining` and `/dhan/ltp` in an explanatory comment. `CloneDhanReadOnly._fetch` intentionally maps Clone 429 cooldown to internal keys `/dhan/index-ltp-batch` and `/dhan/selected-ltp-batch`. These are **in-memory compatibility keys only**, not `urllib` URLs; they may confuse source grep/readers, but no evidence they redirect network requests to V1.
- **Oracle Clone backend source:** `backend/clone_v2_gateway.py` defines only `/clone-v2/market/quotes` and `/clone-v2/market/candles` with separate Clone token and index `SENSEX=("BSE_I",51)`; has no `/dhan/index-ltp-batch` handler. Source nginx snippet `ops/clone_v2_nginx_location.conf` specifies `location ^~ /clone-v2/ { proxy_pass http://127.0.0.1:8002; }`, whereas V1 local service has port 8000. Separate exact OAuth `/dhan/callback` points to Clone 8002; it is login callback, **not quote or candle endpoint**. Legacy V1 `/dhan/...` routes may remain live by design to keep old V1 running; their presence alone cannot hijack a `/clone-v2/market/quotes` request if deployed nginx reflects the designed configuration.
- **LIMIT:** Current Oracle process/nginx effective config, actual DNS/TLS route, deployed gateway blob, HTTP SENSEX response and fixed-enum diagnostic logs were **not inspected live**: GitHub repo contains deployment templates only. Therefore no live cross-service misrouting was confirmed or excluded; no SSH/session tool is currently available to execute remote commands from this chat.
- Created owner-operator safe **read-only** Oracle audit `ops/audit_v3_sensex_v1_route_isolation_readonly.sh` (commits `3b9280883cca715785ebabb4796b3ac0df3ac94a`, `94d2f5d48ea4bc47ba14c85018b2351c628c8b09`). Checks local V1 vs Clone health ports, classifies only effective nginx `/clone-v2/`, old `/dhan/` and OAuth callback proxy targets, and extracts ONLY safe fixed-enum `route=quotes|candles symbol=SENSEX stage=... category=...` from existing Clone journal. Does not print nginx contents, headers, keys, tokens, raw Dhan response, secrets; sends **zero** provider calls and performs **zero** mutations. Needs explicit operator to run via SSH; log returns can prove which route is deployed/where SENSEX actually fails. If absent permission/diagnostics, mark INCONCLUSIVE.
- **P0 conclusion:** Existing V3 source has no confirmed legacy V1 SENSEX request path; suspected issue now concentrates on *deployed nginx/service route* or *Clone Dhan index quote/historical provider response/auth/entitlement*, not changed mapping or 1m timeframe. ### 2026-11-22 — Watchlist slow refresh / full linkage supervision (READ-ONLY source audit)
- Owner reports Watchlist takes over 60–90 seconds to appear up to date and requests faster architecture with no rate-limit errors; asks whether every end-to-end linkage is actually successful. Inspected current branch HEAD `a8a63e4fabea76523571bbb068dc7153f7e5bc23`, latest relevant `src/main.py`, `src/clone_v2_gateway.py`, `backend/clone_v2_gateway.py`, existing exact offline result [44978905825](https://github.com/vaishnavi596/volume-king/actions/runs/44978905825) 1556/1556 PASS. **No new device/provider latency telemetry in this audit**; hence no measured fresh-phone latency or claim 100% live wired.
- **Source diagnosis:** `src/main.py:refresh_watch_batch` uses `priority_equity_batch(all_watch_symbols(), active_paper,...)` and `dhan_feed.equity_quotes_for_watchlist` once to fetch selected equity LTP, merges valid response and retains prior values on partial failure. Phone `CloneDhanReadOnly._fetch` serializes quote calls with 1.15-second spacing and stage-aware 2s/30s 429 cooldown; gateway `/clone-v2/market/quotes` validates 1–73 total/at most 70 equities, uses exact-selection cache TTL 20s or full 3-index 60s, enforces 1.05s shared lane and provider quota cooldown. Correctness safeguard: do NOT fan out quote calls per symbol or remove 429 cooldown.
- **Plausible watchlist >1m bottleneck identified in independent candle path:** `src/main.py:watch_login_candle_bootstrap` walks up to 70 missing 3m histories sequentially; each uncached symbol sleeps 1.5s before `get_market_data(sym,"1m","2d")` with 25s timeout, plus scanner/index/warmup contention and individual `/dhan/intraday` cooldown. Just scheduled sleeps alone total 15s for 10, 60s for 40, 105s for 70 entirely cache-missing equities, excluding network/processing time. Thus 60–90s *candle completion* is plausible from code; it is NOT evidence that a single quote batch takes this long. `dhan_quote_poll` starts after 10s and has a 60s minimum automatic polling interval, with session/cooldown guards; manual refresh has 30s throttle; scan's cached batch reuse TTL 45s. Distinguish quote freshness from historical readiness, on-screen repaint and broker delivery latency.
- **Design decision for next controlled implementation:** use a single-generation deduplicated selected-equity quote batch/cache, display retained prices instantly with explicit age/unverified label, refresh prices separately from candles, prioritize visible/selected symbols and active paper positions before background historical tasks, reuse validated 1m history for complete aligned 3m groups, incremental UI row updates without remount/scroll-triggered Dhan calls, do not allow lower-priority scanner/history to block foreground selected Watchlist; shared bounded/paced history budget with fairness and cancellation on generation changes, backoff from real 429, preserve last-good and diagnostic start→gateway→cache→UI traces. Explicitly avoid uncontrolled parallel Dhan calls, 70-per-symbol quote fanout and fabricated data. Test quote batch timing, cache hits/partial, 5/34/70 symbols, contention, 429, source/timeframe integrity and UI first-visible latency before declaring improvement.

### 25 नवंबर 2026 — बिंदु 1: Dhan कैंडल त्रुटि में HTTP स्थिति खोने का सुधार (Claude)
- **समस्या:** B210 Doctor रिपोर्ट में पाँचों `FeedError` का `http_status` `UNKNOWN_NOT_RECORDED` आया, इसलिए गेटवे बनाम Dhan की जड़ तय नहीं हो पा रही थी।
- **जड़ (कोड से पुष्ट):** `src/main.py::remember_candle_failure` HTTP संख्या `safe_error(exc)` के सुरक्षित पाठ से खोज रहा था। `safe_error` (`src/feed_health.py`) 502/503/504 वाले संदेश, जैसे "Dhan upstream unavailable or token expired (HTTP 502)" और "Dhan server HTTP 502", को "Dhan upstream temporarily unavailable; retry later" में बदल देता है, जिसमें संख्या नहीं रहती। इसलिए खोज हमेशा खाली लौटती थी।
- **सुधार:** नया शुद्ध फ़ंक्शन `src/feed_health.py::http_status_from_exception(exc)` मूल त्रुटि से केवल 400–599 की संख्या लौटाता है (`HTTPError.code` या `HTTP 4xx/5xx` पैटर्न)। कच्चा त्रुटि-पाठ, URL या टोकन कभी नहीं लौटता। `remember_candle_failure` अब इसी का उपयोग करता है। उपयोगकर्ता को दिखने वाला संदेश नहीं बदला।
- **टेस्ट:** नया व्यवहार-परीक्षण `tests/test_v3_http_status_from_exception.py` (4 टेस्ट, पास): 502/503/504 के दोनों असली संदेश, 429 और 403, `urllib HTTPError`, और बिना संख्या या गुप्त-पाठ वाली त्रुटि में `None`। संबंधित 35 मौजूदा टेस्ट स्थानीय रूप से पास हुए। `tests/test_remaining_filter_batch.py` स्थानीय रूप से नहीं चल सका (flet इंस्टॉल नहीं); उसका नतीजा B3 से लेना है।

## 25 नवंबर 2026 — OWNER APPROVED: SMART 3.0 TURBO COMPACT (अनिवार्य)
यह वर्तमान कार्यप्रणाली SMART 2.0 को supersede करती है; पुराने सुरक्षा, सत्यापन और source-isolation नियम बरकरार हैं।
पाँच चरण: REVERSE SCAN → JOINT FIX → JOINT TEST → AUTO CONTINUE → FINAL CLOSE.
सात नियम: ZERO SKIP, ZERO INCOMPLETE, ZERO DUPLICATE, ZERO IDLE (उपलब्ध सक्रिय सत्र में), MAXIMUM JOINT WORK, MINIMUM CI COST, MINIMUM TALK.
**STRICT DEPENDENCY CLOSURE / NO BYPASS:** जिस कार्यसमूह को शुरू किया है, उसके सभी आवश्यक dependencies (app, server/gateway, Dhan source, owner phone/install/permissions/report, wiring, tests, evidence) का वास्तविक समापन और verification किए बिना आगे का dependent कार्य या अगला मुख्य Batch शुरू नहीं होगा। प्रत्येक dependency को owner/AI/server, exact action, evidence और PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED/PENDING से register करें। Owner/server action जरूरी हो तो एक बार स्पष्ट, संक्षिप्त निर्देश दें; उसी gate पर BLOCKED रखें; समाधान का प्रमाण जाँचकर ही खोलें। बिना अनुमति server production, credentials या real orders न बदलें। BLOCKED को PASS, skip या defer करके batch बंद न करें।
एक सुरक्षित संयुक्त source+test+journal commit; fail-fast targeted tests फिर आवश्यक exact-SHA full gate; Workflow चलते समय केवल non-conflicting read-only तैयारी, नया competing push नहीं। वास्तविक logs और फोन evidence से closure; छह मूल ML5 Batch ही रखें। प्रत्येक batch की सभी acceptance rows PASS तभी CLOSED; अन्यथा OPEN/BLOCKED। बार-बार OK नहीं; सिर्फ आवश्यक user action, unresolved blocker या completed batch की compact report। Chat session के बाहर autonomous AI coding उपलब्ध मानकर न चलें। GitHub automated controller अभी प्रस्तावित, implementation अलग सत्यापन के अधीन।

- Current C3 source e5c4884 CI status अलग से सत्यापित करना बाकी। यह docs-only policy update है, न नया source/APK/server change, न controller implementation.
